## `AFG-8121-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Glossmeter-Specular Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Pendulum Friction Coefficient Slip Resistance & ATP Hygiene
- **Intro:** Glossmeter-Specular Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Pendulum Friction Coefficient Slip Resistance & ATP Hygiene — Pre-Calibration Environment Verification: Temperature: 20±2°C; Relative Humidity: 50±10%; Light intensity: <500 lux at the measurement surface.
- **Key tolerance:** Temperature: 20±2°C; Relative Humidity: 50±10%; Light intensity: <500 lux at the measurement surface.
- **Standards:** `ISO 5725-1:1994`, `ISO 9001:2015`, `ASTM E303-22 Standard Test Method for Measuring Surface Friction`, `ISO 18593:2018`
